# Graph Report - gobi-social  (2026-10-20)

## Corpus Check
- 9503 files · ~21,290,385 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.
- Unclassified: 529 file(s) not represented in the graph (top: .woff2 285, (none) 60, .ttf 51)

## Summary
- 122098 nodes · 312003 edges · 3681 communities (3159 shown, 522 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 94% EXTRACTED · 6% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 19702 edges (avg confidence: 0.86)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `e6179a11`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
